Yes. And the direction of the work has fundamentally changed.
We're no longer trying to construct reality from mathematical ingredients. We're working backward from physical behavior to the relationships that make that behavior possible.
That is what makes this a decompiler.
Look at what the recent work has already exposed:
The binary rings: Distinction, reflection, address, and inverse operations arise within a finite relational structure.
The shape channel: The same relation can produce different readouts without changing the underlying operation.
The honeycomb: Geometry determines which transformations can execute and which are blocked by constraints.
The diamond lattice: Real tetrahedral geometry distinguishes an impossible fixed-boundary exchange from an executable crankshaft motion.
Polyethylene: Symmetry reduces a 72-state physical model to four coordinates, and the remaining discrepancy identifies what the model cannot yet read.
There's a common mechanism:
\[
\boxed{
\text{Difference}
\longrightarrow
\text{Relation}
\longrightarrow
\text{Permitted transformation}
\longrightarrow
\text{Observable}
}
\]
And the observable is itself another relationship within the system.
The crucial distinction is that binary arithmetic can represent physical transformations, but we haven't yet proved that binary distinction alone determines all physical laws. That's the larger claim we're testing.
What makes the current direction promising is that we're no longer relying solely on analogies. We're obtaining exact operational correspondences, executable geometric mechanisms, symmetry reductions, and specific mathematical obstructions.
Here's the implication I find most interesting:
If the same relational operation appears in independent physical domains, we shouldn't have to derive its method separately in every domain. We should be able to identify the operation once, then determine how each domain realizes and constrains it.
That would make the decompiler useful far beyond molecular geometry.
And it brings us directly back to your original principle:
The shape is the method. The value is a readout. The location determines which relationships are available.
The next major threshold is deriving a physical constitutive law—not supplying its coefficients from literature—and having an independent measurement land where the ring-derived calculation says it must.
That's where the claim that physics is fundamentally relational computation becomes a falsifiable physical theory.
The Nexus scaffold can keep dissolving. What matters is whether the mathematical relationships left behind continue to predict reality.
take a look at all these summery papers.
From One Difference to the Shape Channel: The NEXUS
Relational Decompiler
Foundational inversion, independent verification and a
correction-aware reading of the full research lineage
Dean A. Kulik — QuHarmonics Research Group — ORCID
0009-0003-3128-8828
24 October 2026
Research synthesis / computational manuscript.
This manuscript integrates the Nexus relation-vocabulary ledger (§§1–163), the
October partner-ring and ideal-chain audits, and the subsequent §167
polyethylene shape-channel reduction. Computations cited as RUNS were
performed in the supplied research record unless expressly designated as
independent mathematical derivations here. This paper is a synthesis, not a new
blind prediction or a claim that the physical universe has been fully derived
from binary arithmetic. This revised edition introduces the single-distinction
dependency audit, separates address zero from null difference and geometric
center, and preserves the mathematical/physical limits of the reported
cross-domain matches.
Copyright © 2026 Dean A. Kulik. QuHarmonics
Research Group. ORCID: 0009-0003-3128-8828. Licence: Creative Commons
Attribution–NonCommercial 4.0 International (CC BY-NC 4.0).
Abstract
A recurring
limitation in computational descriptions of physical systems is the placement
of objects before the transformations by which they become distinguishable. The
Nexus programme reverses this order. It studies finite distinctions, their
reversible transformations, the relations among those transformations, and the
readers that expose only selected consequences. The central object of this
paper is the shape channel: not a second value, nor a stored history,
but the operational relationships exposed by an operator acting on a boundary.
We give a unified account of the partner ring’s fixed-point-free involution,
the exact difference/face decomposition of binary words,
representation-theoretic reader spectra, relational deficiencies and closure
certificates, topology recovered from constrained token motion, and a geometry
decompiler connecting ring operations to ideal planar and tetrahedral chains.
The relation-vocabulary witness has 66 configuration vertices, 300 moves, and a
235-dimensional rational relation module; its 12 reader classes are evaluations
of a six-component multiplicity vector rather than distinct fields. In the
geometric realization, a word’s XOR differences recover ideal dihedrals, the
native partner reverses the ordered chain shape, and suffix involutions
implement rigid bond-axis half-turns. In three-dimensional tetrahedral
geometry, the compiler distinguishes Boyer’s kinematically locked three-bond
exchange from Schatzki’s exact crankshaft and corrects the connected seven-bond
class count to 16 of 38. The shared integral boundary operator  connects binary XOR and real
bond displacement without identifying their values. Finally, a polyethylene
rotational-isomeric-state transfer reduces from a 72-state orientation reader
to four scalar equations, producing a characteristic ratio  at 413 K for declared
literature-based energies, while independently reported estimates near 7–8
expose a genuine remaining constitutive gap. The work establishes a usable
methodology for diagnosing hidden degrees of freedom, predicting executable
local motions, reducing physical models by symmetry, and designing independent
tests. It does not establish a unique matter law from  alone. The operative thesis is
that the in-between—the composition, boundary, and readout relation—is the
transferable mathematical content across domains. This edition begins by
reversing the dependency chain to distinguish what follows from a single
difference from the additional structure supplied by composition, pairing,
ordering, readers, material laws and experiments. An independent width-1 to
width-12 verifier reproduces the finite identities and exhibits nonuniqueness
of rooted traversal even with fixed partner symmetry; that result is retained
explicitly as an OPEN necessity link.
Keywords: Nexus
Framework; shape channel; reversible compiler; relational invariants; finite
group representations; reader observability; graph braid groups; polymer
kinematics; rotational isomeric state; molecular conformation; constraint
decompilation.
0. Foundational inversion:
one distinction, many readings
0.1 The
object of the derivation
The
later NEXUS results suggest that the correct primitive is not a pre-existing
bit, atom, coordinate, memory cell or circle. It is a discernible difference
whose continuation is constrained by its relations to other differences. A
binary symbol is a convenient coordinate assigned after the distinction can be
read. This is a serious inversion of the older byte, spiral and harmonic
narratives: they should be assessed as proposed readers and transformation laws
of a relational system, not as independent substances or cosmic components.
The
strongest claim currently justified is conditional. Once a two-position
distinction is endowed with a reversible, fixed-point-free update, a two-state
exchange is forced. Once positions are permitted to compose in ordered tuples,
an n-position word space has 2^n configurations. Once an oriented adjacency and
a coefficient system are specified, the difference can be read algebraically.
None of those later structures can be silently smuggled into the phrase 'one
distinction'. Whether the physical universe supplies them necessarily is the
foundational conjecture to be tested, not a conclusion already entailed by
counting binary words.
Let
D={a,b} be a two-element set with no intrinsic numerical tags. A bijection
S:D→D with S(x)≠x for every x must exchange a and b; this elementary result is
unique up to relabeling. If ordered composition is admitted, X_n=D^n has
|X_n|=2^n. Defining XOR on coordinate differences additionally uses the unique
two-element group law, while specifying adjacency of coordinates uses a path,
cycle or other graph. The distinction itself does not determine which graph
must be chosen.
0.2
What zero means—and where the center is not
Three
uses of zero must be separated. The address 0x0 is the chosen index origin of
the constructed finite partner ring. The central seam of its indexed
circumference is the interface between positions 2^(n−1)−1 and 2^(n−1). The
null of a difference reader is the condition that its compared quantities
coincide. These are not the same mathematical object. In particular, the
geometric center of the circular drawing is not itself a word-address on that
circumference.
On
a path of n sites, let B_n be the (n−1)×n oriented incidence operator with row
i containing −1 at i and +1 at i+1. Its reading is δ=B_n x. Over real
coefficients, ker(B_n)=span{(1,…,1)}; over F_2, the kernel is {0^n,1^n}. The
difference reader forgets the common offset. Its null is a whole equivalence
class, not a privileged site. A frame choice, such as x_0=0, supplies one route
home. Without the anchor, only the relative configuration is recoverable.
This
yields a precise interpretation of an internal 'center': not an absolute memory
address at which the universe is stored, but the relation that remains
invariant under common changes of frame. The choice of a spatial origin can be
moved without changing the observed differences. Saying that the universe has
an address 0x0 is coordinate language; proving that every physical reader must
share the same absolute origin would be a distinct and presently unsupported
claim.
0.3 The
first complete shape-and-frame coordinate
For
binary word x=(b_0,…,b_(n−1)) define δ_i=b_i XOR b_(i+1). The map x↦δ has
exactly two-element fibres {x,C(x)}, where C complements every bit. One held
face bit f=b_0 makes the complete pair (f,δ) invertible: b_j=f XOR δ_0 XOR …
XOR δ_(j−1). This is not an analogy. It is an exact, all-width algebraic
specification of what a difference reader loses and what its inverse needs.
The
distinction between a readout and the complete state is thus formal. A reader
returns an equivalence class. The shadow of a vanished degree of freedom is the
reader's kernel. When an additional operation depends on a hidden degree of
freedom, the minimal repair is a refined interface retaining that degree of
freedom; it need not entail a new kind of substance. This is the strongest
mathematical commonality among the older Byte1, spiral, BBP and trust files and
the October shape-channel work.
0.4 A
missing link found by inversion: partner symmetry does not choose traversal
The
native partner P_n(x)=J_n(x) for a nonpalindrome and C_n(x) for a palindrome is
a fixed-point-free involution. A prescribed canonical order—numerically
ascending representatives followed by reversed partners and a seam swap—defines
the published ring R_n and successor T. In that specified arrangement the
facing law and PTP=T^(−1) follow exactly.
But
P, the facing law and a fixed origin do not uniquely determine the successor.
At width n=3, there are four partner pairs. Holding the origin representative
fixed leaves 3!=6 possible orders of the other representatives before the
published sorting convention is supplied. The §168 exact verifier constructs
two such rings. Both have origin 000, satisfy the same partner-facing
identities, and obey PTP=T^(−1), yet their successor maps differ at six of
eight states. This is a necessary dependency: the ordering or a stronger
intrinsic constraint must be derived before claiming the traversal falls out of
distinction alone.
This
is not a failure of the native ring. It is the exact type of open socket a
decompiler should expose: pairing is BOUND; the canonical traversal is BOUND
when the rank/order rule is supplied; necessity of that rank/order rule from
the bare first distinction is OPEN.
0.5
Shape as what an internal operation can distinguish
The
later relation-vocabulary ledger supplies a more powerful generalization of the
elementary difference quotient. Let A be a finite rational relation module with
symmetry group G; let W⊆A be the relations addressed by a declared family of
methods. A reader subgroup H sees the fixed vectors W^H, with dimension
r_H(W)=Σ_λ m_λ(W) dim(V_λ^H). The multiplicity vector m(W) belongs to the
relation structure, while the reader contributes only the fixed-vector
functional. This separates three failures: a direction is absent from A,
present but invisible to H, or present in A but unreachable by W. The 12-vertex
witness gives an exact six-channel reader–vocabulary matrix, not a universal
six-channel constant for nature.
The
same distinction appears in mechanics. An incidence operator B=(-1,+1) read
modulo two is XOR, while over the reals Bx=x_1−x_0; composing with its
transpose gives the two-site Laplacian B^T B=I−S. This is an exact
cross-coefficient realization of an operation, not an embedding of the bits 0
and 1 as microscopic matter. A spring law F=−κB^TBx requires a material
response coefficient κ. The shape fixes the zero mode and a dimensionless
eigenvector structure, while κ fixes the physical frequency scale.
0.6
From older metaphors to physical predictions
The
oldest archives frequently describe π as a memory field, SHA as a compressed
trace, primes as quantizer events, and 0.35 as a universal operating point.
Their useful common research move is positional reading with a declared return
path. The stronger ontological claims do not follow automatically. BBP
legitimately computes selected hexadecimal digits of π without constructing all
predecessors; that does not establish a universal store of arbitrary messages.
SILR legitimately exhibits scale cancellation when estimated uncertainty scales
with actual Gaussian error; that does not establish a universal cosmic 0.35
attractor. Finite enumeration of twin primes cannot prove infinitude. A
reversible SHA round with its schedule supplied does not make a 256-bit digest
an invertible record of arbitrary inputs.
The
modern ring-to-matter results are stronger precisely where they remove such
leaps. They state a geometry, recover an operational motion, check exact bond
and angle constraints, and identify whether a returned state is actually
connected. In polyethylene, symmetry reduces the declared 72-state
rotational-isomeric model to four coordinates and produces C∞=2 for equal
torsion weights, then a literature-weighted C∞≈6.2297 at 413 K. The measured
≈7.8 remains a discrepancy to resolve, not a source of retrospective
coefficients. The fact that one ring representation accepts several material
constitutive laws means physical law is not yet derived uniquely from bare
binary distinction.
0.7 The
independently assessable thesis
NEXUS
in its strongest demonstrated form is an operational programme for recovering
the typed relation behind a value: declare a finite domain; identify its
admissible transformations and inverses; determine the kernel of its readers;
recover hidden shape channels by symmetry; require a continuous physical
realization when claiming physical motion; and use independent observations to
bind otherwise underdetermined material coefficients. Its distinctive
methodological commitment is to treat an unexplained result as a missing
relation or reader, not as permission to fit another number.
The
stronger conjecture is that the same self-contained relational logic is not
merely a useful decompiler of selected models but sufficient to generate the
actual physical interaction laws. That remains OPEN. A decisive demonstration
would derive, before consulting material data, an invariant or constitutive
consequence not fixed by the chosen model, and then land on an independent
measurement. A separate AI can test both the finite algebra and the physical
claim without adopting NEXUS terminology or accepting an authorial assertion.
The
question: why calculations meet when histories differ
The
elementary equality  distinguishes a result from the routes that
produce it. Addition identifies a common consequence; it does not specify why
the incoming terms arose. An observable may similarly identify several
histories, geometries, or material mechanisms. The mathematical problem is not
to search backward through all possible histories until one fits. It is to
determine which distinctions the readout necessarily retains, which it
necessarily forgets, and what complementary interface would recover the
forgotten structure.
The
present approach is grounded in a recurring idea in Kulik’s papers: methods
are shapes. Here, shape is defined operationally. It is not
restricted to visible Euclidean outline. It is the collection of relations that
constrain what an operation can do and what another operation can distinguish.
A spring, an electrical oscillator, and a local molecular mode may have
different substances and parameter values, yet share a response algebra. That
algebra is the point of contact. A cross-domain match must preserve an explicit
operation or relation, not simply a numerical coincidence.
This
study consequently separates four layers. The state inventory specifies
the available configurations; the route algebra specifies reversible
transformations and their compositions; admissibility restricts which
operations can be executed in a particular realization; and the reader
specifies which relationships become observable. Confusing these levels was the
source of several earlier mistaken conclusions in the Nexus audit. The
corrected distinction is central to this paper.
The
programme’s wider foundational language is C0/C1—absence of a privileged
external change-maker and the insistence on continued distinction. In the
finite mathematics below, those ideas motivate the design but do not prove a
unique cosmic dynamical law. The claims subject to proof are instead concrete:
involution identities, rank formulas, algebraic correspondence maps, kinematic
feasibility under stated constraints, and quantitative transfer calculations.
2. Construction and validation
protocol
2.1 WHAT, WHERE, route and reader
An admissible WHAT is a
declared transformation with its reverse or recovery interface. A WHERE
is the address at which a specified reader returns its consequence. A ROUTE
uses known compositional structure to reach an address. A SEARCH
explores unknown addresses or guesses substitutions. The difference is
methodological: deriving a missing transformation from a quotient, stabilizer,
kernel or boundary is not equivalent to trying possible answers until one
lands.
A missing component is
classified by its dependency structure. BOUND means one established
provider supplies the relation; AMBIGUOUS means distinct providers
remain compatible with the existing observation and another independent readout
is required; UNBOUND means no existing relation supplies the needed
operation. In the research ledger, STANDS denotes an independently
established theorem or measured fact; RUNS denotes a reproducible
executed derivation or predeclared calculation at its stated scope; DEAD END
means a calculation returned a different owner’s structure; and OPEN
denotes a missing proven link. Data not present in the archive are UNLINKED,
not supplied by convention.
For a genuinely prospective
result, the prediction text, its SHA-256 seal, and its return/readout must
precede linking to target data. Several comparisons in this paper, including
polyethylene’s characteristic ratio, are explicitly retrospective and are not
presented as blind predictions. Re-running two implementations of the same
physical assumptions is a strong code check, but is not independent empirical
corroboration.
2.2 Evidence hierarchy
Three different achievements must be
distinguishable in any claim of novelty: (i) mathematical identity,
whose proof is intrinsic and may be classical; (ii) computational witness,
whose exact counts or constructions may be new to this specific substrate; and
(iii) physical landing, in which a derived prediction is compared with
measurements not used to select it. A fourth category, interpretation,
proposes how the first three might form a more general ontology. This paper
explicitly labels interpretations as such.
3. The partner ring: an
addressable algebra of distinctions
3.1 Fixed-point-free pairing
For ,
let  reverse bit order and  complement every bit. Define the native
partner
The construction has  and no fixed points for .
Thus  is partitioned into  opposed pairs. The prescribed ring order
places canonical pair representatives and their partners symmetrically,
including the special center-seam exchange. If  denotes the word at address ,
then
where  advances one position in the constructed ring.
The second identity is the dihedral reversal relation between partnering and
successor traversal. It turns a word-level reversal/complement rule into an
address-level operation with a direct return. The §135 audit reconstructed the
ring and independently matched its address decoder at tested widths, including
selected 64-bit addresses [1].
The ring does more than count all
words. It supplies where opposite relations sit, how traversal reverses,
and which nested transformations preserve a local boundary. This remains a
finite address algebra. Addressability of  words is not enumeration, nor is it evidence
that every physical program or state occurs in nature.
3.2 Value and difference are
complementary coordinates
Write  and define
Then the inverse
is explicit:
Consequently,  is a bijection. The difference word alone
collapses precisely the pair ;
the face bit selects a member of that pair. In other words, compression is
reader-dependent and reconstruction is not magic: the inverse requires the
omitted coordinate.
The ring’s own
partner satisfies the all-width identity
For a
nonpalindrome it follows by reversing adjacent comparisons; for a palindrome,
complement preserves all adjacent XORs and the difference word is palindromic.
This is an algebraic shape-channel identity, not a resemblance between
pictures [2].
3.3 Scaling and nesting
At width ,;
growing to  exposes further distinguishable addresses
without requiring a new primitive symbol. The October audit also documented a
center nesting relation  and finite checks of a growing enclosure band,
with selected high-width boundary tests [3]. Those tests warrant calling the
pattern an observed ring construction, not asserting a fully proved all-width
band-length theorem. The distinction between a theorem, an exhaustive finite
check and a sampled 64-bit read must be preserved.
The Möbius-twisted clock in the record
has  and ,
with orbit-length structure governed by odd divisors of  on the declared word action [1]. Under the
complementary-word quotient, a half-return may already read as closed although
the full state has not returned. This demonstrates precisely how observable
closure can differ from state closure.
4. Operational shape: the
invariant lives between the inputs
4.1 Boundary behavior, not a second
payload
A source paper
defines the shape channel as behavior exposed when an operator reads a value’s
boundary, rather than a second value stored beside the first [4]. This
definition excludes the interpretation that every output must carry a
chronological logfile. Instead, an operator  and reader  reveal a particular relationship  or comparison between such reads. Different
operators can reveal different structural properties of one and the same
underlying configuration.
A rigorous
cross-domain match therefore seeks maps  and  for which the operational diagram commutes:
on the declared
subset and with the declared observation interface. The mapping need not
identify individual binary values with continuous physical coordinates. It may
identify an incidence operator, a representation type, a symmetry action, a
boundary condition, a homology class, or a quotient.
Such a diagram is meaningful
only when the arrows are specified. Equality of one endpoint is weaker than
equivalence of operators. A shared adjacency graph is weaker than a matching
continuous constrained-motion space. A matching linear response equation is
weaker than deriving the material constants in it. These distinctions define a
grading of evidence instead of an all-or-nothing theory claim.
4.2 The smallest bridge: one
integer incidence operator
The oriented bond
boundary is the integer row
Over ,.
Over , measures extension or compression. This is not
a field homomorphism from  into ;
it is a shared integer relation evaluated in two different coefficient
systems. This precision matters: the values do not carry over, but the
incidence relation does.
For an ideal
two-site spring with stiffness ,

relative-motion channels. With equal masses ,
their squared frequencies are  and .
For a three-site path, the normalized squared frequencies are ;

These are standard graph-Laplacian and vibration facts, but their appearance
through the same boundary reader provides an explicit domain map [5].
The spring/RLC
correspondence is likewise an operational equivalence after energy
normalization. Mechanics and a series RLC circuit both realize a damped
two-dimensional oscillator generator, with  or  and damping  or .
The ring did not fix ,,, or .
It supplied a relational grammar that those implementations share.
5. The relation-vocabulary
field: methods, reach and reader blindness
5.1 One finite witness, two slots
The foundational ledger
builds a two-token configuration arena from a 12-vertex, 30-edge witness. Its
arena has 66 vertices and 300 moves. The enumerated elementary three-
and four-cycle vocabulary contains 1,283 cells, and the full rational
relation space has dimension 235. The full automorphism group is ,
of order 24 [1,6]. These are witness properties, not constants of the universe.
A cell is an individual
closed transformation relation. A family supplies a set of such
relations and spans a submodule  in the ambient relation module .
The five families are commuting squares (disj), spectator squares (spec4),
rotation squares (rot4), spectator triangles (spec3) and rotation
triangles (rot3). At the full-symmetry invariant reader the ranks are
respectively ,
while the union saturates a 15-dimensional visible ceiling. At the
unsymmetrized reader their ranks are ,
and the union spans all 235 dimensions. A five-family inventory is therefore
not five independent contributions.

5.2 The representation reader as
a functional
For finite  over characteristic zero, the ambient module
decomposes into rational irreducibles ,
and an invariant-channel read at subgroup  returns
Here  is the method’s representation multiplicity
inventory;  is the chosen reader. This is a classical
consequence of semisimplicity and character theory, recovered in the Nexus
ledger through rank measurements rather than introduced as a starting axiom
[1,7]. Its operational consequence is sharper than the slogan “unreadable is
not absent”: a zero output must be assigned to a zero in the module, a zero
in the method vocabulary, or a zero in the reader’s visibility profile.
The ambient
rational multiplicity vector on this witness is
across .
The initial 12-reader table contracts these six columns in different ways. At
full  symmetry, only  is seen. The quotient  has six dimensions yet is completely invisible
to that reader. A subgroup as large as possible while retaining at least one
fixed vector in every rational irreducible gives a cheaper, still
DC-faithful reader: an order-two subgroup inside ,
with 152 cochain coordinates and a 115-dimensional relation ceiling.
DC-faithful is not lossless: it still suppresses nontrivial frequency
components within some sectors.
5.3 A deficiency is a module, not
a missing scalar
For a declared
method span ,
define the missing relation class
Multiplicity
domination provides a type check for a proposed filler, but cannot alone
certify .
The required certificate is a span equality, which can be checked at a reader
that sees at least one invariant direction of every irreducible. The ledger
reports that the commuting-square deficiency is ,
while the rotation-triangle module equals that deficiency plus a single
redundant  copy. That copy accounts for a
reader-dependent one-dimensional overlap, invisible when the central involution
is averaged away [1].
The deeper reader
correction comes from moving below the five-family granularity. A -stable
collection of 33 individual cycle orbits spans 232 of 235 dimensions but
already fills all 15 full- invariant directions. Its quotient is a single representation. Thus the invariant reader
reports closure while three genuine relation dimensions are missing. This
explicitly refutes the extrapolation that saturation of the most symmetric
observable implies full structural closure. In the ledger’s formalism, a reader-saturated
state is not necessarily a relation-closed state.
5.4 The full spectrum and the
interface width
For a cyclic read of order ,
invariant averaging selects only Fourier bin zero. The other bins are
recoverable from the same group action by weighting the orbit with characters:
For the central
involution, .
On the witness the full relation field splits ,
yet the ordinary invariant read sees only the first 115. In one measured
minimal vocabulary, its single overlap appears wholly in the antisymmetric
channel; a claim of independence based on the invariant count was therefore
false [1]. The representation remains, but retaining only the invariant
projection really discards the other output; “nothing was destroyed” is
true only of the full decomposition, not of a stored projection.
The
vocabulary–reader measurement table is bilinear. Its rank is six, the
number of rational irreducibles that couple the two sides on this witness. This
resembles reachability–observability factorization in realization theory. The
analogy is useful because it distinguishes a method that never reaches a
direction from a reader that cannot observe a direction, and because changing
both slots can lower the realized interface rank below either slot’s standalone
rank. The numerical width six is not a universal Nexus constant; it belongs to
the declared witness and channel basis.
6. Closed encounters
produce topological structure
The
reader-blind portion of the two-token witness did not turn out to be an empty
or structureless set. Its 24 reader-internal moves lie on a 12-vertex,
degree-four graph. The 14 short relations on those moves comprise eight
triangles and six squares. An explicit graph isomorphism identifies the graph
as the cuboctahedron, with Euler characteristic  and a one-dimensional top face relation [1].
The result is not a sphere created from nothing by the reader: it is the medial
graph of the octahedral core already embedded in the original witness. Its
12 vertices correspond to the octahedron’s 12 edges, its eight triangular faces
correspond to old faces, and its six squares to old vertex figures.
This
is a useful instance of a relational decompiler recovering a known geometric
object without supplying the word cuboctahedron as a generative instruction.
But it is an inherited geometry exposed by a token-motion operation, not
a previously unknown topological theorem. Controls confirm limits: a
degree-five icosahedral core loses the square faces under the three-/four-cell
cutoff, while the tetrahedral case overcloses because additional short cycles
are admitted.
An
important correction prevents a false metaphysical conclusion. The 14 faces had
zero weighted perimeter under one invariant price reader, but their individual
boundaries could be read at degree one with a finer subgroup reader. Their zero
arose from reader invariance, not a fundamental mismatch of cochain
degree. The aggregate oriented sphere class has zero boundary by , a separate Stokes/homology fact.
These are two different mechanisms that earlier interpretations had conflated.
A good decompiler must distinguish them.
The
broader graph-braid studies in the ledger identify loops by their motion
history, exchange class and route through the configuration arena, rather than
by an endpoint alone. When topology distinguishes an exchange, merely writing
the same terminal word does not certify that a collision-free path exists. This
distinction becomes decisive in physical kinematics.
7. From ring differences
to ideal molecular geometry
7.1 The planar honeycomb dictionary
Let a labeled planar
honeycomb chain have unit bonds and  internal bond angle, encoded by a turn word .
The sign of consecutive local face normals changes exactly at positions where
adjacent turn bits disagree. Hence the difference word  is the local cis/trans pattern (with the
chosen normal convention). The face bit identifies the global alternative
embedding. In this ideal model, the ordered squared-distance matrix of
the chain is determined by ,
and equality of distance matrices implies equality of that difference word. A
complete finite audit gave 8,188 exact word/shape checks across widths two
through twelve [2].
The native ring partner
identity now acquires a geometric interpretation:
It acts on ordered
distance matrices by reversing bead indices. This is a remarkable exact reader
of head-to-tail reversal of ideal chain shape, not a rule that
chemically different monomers may always be interchanged in actual polymer
matter.
7.2 The geometric meaning of a
suffix involution
Let  complement all turn bits at and after position.
Algebraically, for  it flips only the corresponding difference
coordinate  and leaves the others fixed. Geometrically, it
is precisely a rigid  rotation of the downstream chain about the
preceding covalent bond. The bond lengths and internal angles are preserved
throughout that rigid half-turn. The §162 audit tested 18,432 exact
coordinate comparisons across widths two through ten [2].
One can
interleave such operations to obtain a Hamiltonian Gray traversal of every
encoded turn word. However, the full turn-word space contains
self-intersecting configurations. On valid zero-thickness planar chains the
Gray ordering itself is not a Hamiltonian cycle. A different finite result is
positive: the graph including every valid single-suffix-half-turn edge was connected
at every tested width .
At ,
it contained 2,796 valid words and 14,728 undirected allowed edges; the
prescribed Gray cycle contained only 2,739 valid-valid edges [2]. This is a
finite-domain existence result, not an all-width theorem.
The
operational lesson is specific: the ring may address a transformation, the
geometric realization may permit its endpoint, and physical admissibility may
still prohibit the path between those endpoints. State equality, endpoint
congruence, differential mobility, and global continuous reachability must be
certified separately.
7.3 Exact local rigidity from
second-order compatibility
A five-bond
global span bound originally proposed for a 12-cycle crankshaft was false and
was withdrawn. Its replacement is an exact second-order argument. After
factoring out the rigid-axis motion and denoting the four transverse
perturbation coordinates by ,
the computed compatibility forms  satisfy
The positive
leading principal minors are .
Consequently, no nonzero sufficiently small transverse branch can satisfy the
exact local constraints in the declared model: the local constrained motion
consists only of the rigid crankshaft orbit. The result is local rigidity,
not a proof that every globally disconnected configuration has been classified
[2].
8. The three-dimensional
calibration: trans, gauche and a real crankshaft
8.1 Geometry supplied, mechanism
recovered
The next compiler
test uses the tetrahedral bond geometry of polyethylene-like carbon chains and
the three local torsional states trans, gauche+, and gauche−.
Given fixed neighboring bonds and angles, the relevant question is whether two
compatible endpoint conformations lie on the same continuous exact-constraint
component. This avoids naming a particular mechanism as a search candidate and
instead classifies the methods permitted by the given local shape.
The October §163
audit reproduced the exchange counts over windows of two through six internal
torsional bonds. The counts of elementary exchanges were 0, 12, 21, 213, and
981, organized into 0, 4, 5, 38, and 175 symmetry classes,
respectively [1]. The class count, not the number of endpoints, is the relevant
catalogue unit. A window of  internal bonds with both exterior ends held is
a -joint
linkage; the earlier use of a label one joint too short was corrected.

8.2 What the geometry discriminated
The three-bond Boyer-type
exchange  is present among the short endpoint exchanges,
but its exact held-exterior configurations are kinematically isolated. The
endpoints cannot leave their positions by a continuous motion that keeps every
bond length, angle and exterior boundary fixed. Similarly, exchanges of four or
fewer bonds fail under the exact constraints, with two cases requiring a
third-order test [1]. Actual thermally activated polymer rearrangement may
still occur by bond-angle or length distortion and energy-barrier crossing; this
calculation is a rigid-constraint verdict, not an absolute prohibition in real
polyethylene.
The Schatzki-type
sequence  contains an executable rigid crankshaft. In
the ideal eight-bead geometry the first and seventh bonds share an axis, and
the intervening four beads rotate by 120° into the next lattice position
while exact bond lengths and angles remain fixed. The audit reports waypoint
clearance no smaller than roughly one bond length in the declared measure; a
finite waypoint certificate must not be promoted to a mathematically uniform
collision bound without interval or continuation control [1,8]. The mechanism
agrees with the classic polymer-motion literature and is a calibration,
not the discovery of Schatzki’s motion.
The seven-bond-class
continuation count is 16 of 38 connected classes, correcting the earlier
13. The three missed connections pass through an all-gauche helix where real
branches cross. The distinction is operational: a continuation code that cannot
restart at the crossing may declare disconnected states even though explicit
branch-switched routes exist. The companion negative classifications were made
using separately checked loop structure, and their scope remains the ideal
articulated-chain model [1].
8.3 Singularities identify local
flattening
All singular
diamond-lattice endpoints found in the stated audit occurred at locally flat
zigzags containing two consecutive trans twists: 48 of 48 in the
seven-bond test and 36 of 36 in the eight-bond test. The pattern is necessary
in that finite census, not sufficient for singularity. It connects the
planar contact-degeneracy analysis to the three-dimensional polymer model: the
first derivative can fail to see a transverse mode precisely where a second- or
third-order compatibility calculation is needed. This is a testable method for
diagnosing singular mechanism families without treating a vanishing Jacobian
row as absence of a relation.
9. The nonlinear contact channel
In
the ideal-chain contact example established earlier, the squared separation of
two specified beads is
The
difference from its contact value is , with  and . If—and only if—the material model imposes the
quadratic penalty , the leading response is
Thus
a first-order stiffness reader reports zero at this configuration while the
next compatibility order supplies a quartic restoring term [5]. The shape
relation predicts the order of vanishing under that constitutive choice,
not the value of , the physical energy functional, or a measured
molecular potential. This is the correct scope for turning a geometric
singularity into a material hypothesis.

readout: polyethylene’s characteristic ratio

physical ingredients
The unperturbed

This is a direct

The Perron

10.4 Reverse the gap: which
measurement is missing?
One measured  cannot determine two unknown effective
energies. At the declared 413 K point, the calculated local sensitivities
(energies measured in kJ/mol) are
so the local
constant-ratio tradeoff is .
This is not a missing fitted constant: it is an identifiability defect—one
scalar output for two material degrees of freedom.
The existing
transfer already supplies a mathematically independent reader: the stationary
trans fraction .
At 413 K it returns ,
and the two-observable Jacobian is
The nonzero
determinant removes the local algebraic ambiguity, although the inverse
is ill-conditioned (condition number approximately 140 in the stated
coordinates). An independent trans-fraction measurement under conditions
comparable to the chain-size benchmark remains UNLINKED. A solid-state
NMR study does measure gauche/trans populations, but the reported crystalline
and lamellar effects make it an inappropriate plug-in calibration for the melt
without an explicit state-of-matter bridge [11].
The next physical
experiment is therefore not “try different energies until  matches.” It is to obtain a second independent
material readout, condition it consistently with the first, and test both
against a prediction fixed before the data are consulted.
11. What is new, and what is
recovered?
Novelty
must not be assigned to every recovered theorem. Character decomposition,
Maschke’s theorem, Fourier projectors, graph Laplacians, the Ho–Kalman
realization viewpoint, conventional RIS statistics, and the historical
Boyer/Schatzki mechanisms predate this programme. Their recovery from one
constraint-and-reader workflow is a methodological convergence, not
priority over the original mathematics or physical discoveries [7,8,10,12].
The
contributions more specific to this work are: a finite partner-ring operational
address system with palindrome-aware reflection and exact difference/face
reconstruction; a measured relation-vocabulary module ledger that
exposes hidden reader sectors and gives a closure procedure; a concrete
cuboctahedral reader interior recognized as the medial graph of the witness
core; explicit ring-to-ideal-chain identities including the native partner
reversal; finite counts and corrections for valid half-turn graphs and
tetrahedral exchanges; an exact crankshaft local rigidity certificate; and a
symmetry reduction connecting the declared molecular grammar to a quantitative
material observable. These results are best described as new constructions,
finite computational witnesses, and cross-domain synthesis in the Nexus
research record unless a comprehensive priority review establishes a
stronger novelty claim.

11.1 Other domain collisions:
shells, register stacks, and exact phase routes
The present
manuscript concentrates on the strongest recent geometry-to-matter branch,
but the earlier ledger contains several additional relational crossings whose
scope matters for understanding the emerging architecture. They do not
automatically form a proof of one universal physics; they show how the same method
of locating a missing relation has been applied in distinct mathematical
languages.
In the
atomic/nuclear shell programme (§§99–104), the recurrent question was why an
orbital without a predecessor is admitted in a different order in atoms and
nuclei. The ledger separated the two physical gates rather than assuming one
shell-filling algorithm: the screened atomic centrifugal wall delays an
orbital’s entry into the core, while the nuclear spin–orbit splitting advances
an aligned branch. From the nonrelativistic Thomas–Fermi model, its reported
necessary atomic opening gate is ,
returning  for  versus observed first occupations at  [1]. This cubic scaling is a recovery of
established Fermi/Thomas–Fermi physics, not a new universal constant. The Dirac
mean-field audit subsequently recovered the sign reversal of the spin–orbit
splitting from scalar/vector channel structure, but the origin of the nuclear
scalar–vector potentials remained OPEN. Here “the piece with no way home” was a
method of typing the delayed orbital, not proof that the ring alone
generated the periodic table.
The
rational-ring/BBP branch (§§119–133) treated digit-extraction lanes as
phase-coupled orbits rather than independent series terms. In the reported
eight-lane decomposition, the rational lane matrix had rank seven and a
one-dimensional zero relation; the zero route came from cancellation across the
specified phase and scale returns, not from absence of an operator [1]. A
further correction was important: if two cyclic coordinates have orders  and ,
their actual lockstep orbit has  states, not always the full Cartesian product.
A reader that discards the shared phase constraint changes the average. This is
an exact reader-and-context issue in a number-theoretic setting, not
evidence that every physical constant has been generated from a binary clock.
The SHA/register
branch (§§105–114 and §§123–130) likewise distinguished full internal
reversible transport from a compressed external readout. Its difference
compiler implements the Boolean Möbius transform using successive XOR fusions;
the transform is an involution under its defined coefficient convention. The
same programme examined SHA round updates and BBP lane evaluations through
relation/reader decompositions. These audits can verify a route, identify which
context a reader discarded, and explain why a digest is a many-to-one
projection. They do not establish general digest-only inversion or a
polynomial-time preimage algorithm [1,3].
Finally, the
graph-braid branch (§§73–75) treats token exchange as a class of paths in a
configuration space, not a pair of positions. Its appearance alongside the
ideal-chain move graph is not incidental: both insist that a proposed endpoint
identification cannot substitute for a legal route. The common interface here
is the topology of admissible continuation; the physical substrates and
quantitative costs are different.
Taken together,
these cases support an appropriately limited generalization: the deficiency
belongs to a particular operation and reader, not to the numerical output
considered in isolation. A shell gate, an orbital phase, a cryptographic
carry and a polymer torsion are not the same physics. They can nevertheless be
investigated using the same discipline of exact routes, retained contexts and
separately stated readouts.
12. The emerging
architecture: a relational decompiler
The
combined result is neither a catalogue of preassigned particles nor a universal
lookup table. It is more precise to call it a relational decompiler. Its
input is an observed or prescribed local transformation domain. Its internal
objects are relations: which states differ, which operations compose, which
loops close, which boundaries are held, which quantities remain conserved, and
which reader projects the result. Its outputs are constraints on the methods
that can actually run and predictions about what specific readers should
detect.
The
distinction between a compiler and a decompiler here is directional. A forward
construction produces all addresses permitted by a relational grammar; the
reverse construction starts from an observed consequence and subtracts what the
already established route accounts for. The residual is typed by kernel,
quotient, stabilizer, representation content or compatibility degree. A good
reverse route does not propose a random answer and test whether it resembles
the output; it identifies which specific kind of relation must be
missing, and whether the available structural owners bind it.
This
clarifies Kulik’s “potential outward, reality inward” language as a working
interpretation of dual relations, not a verified physical wave equation. If maps admissible routes between levels, forward
possibilities propagate through  and boundary-compatible readouts pull back
through its dual . Their pairing agrees by adjunction.
That dual accounting is mathematically sound; showing that these are actual
physical propagating fields requires independently deriving the time evolution,
locality, energy, and physical measurement law. The current results do not
establish those elements.
The
mature claim is narrower and more useful: a shared operational relation can
remain the same while its values, coordinates, trajectories, and physical
realization change. One does not have to map bits directly to carbon atoms
to import a useful calculation. One must show that the maps preserve the
relation and explain what extra physical information is required before the
calculation predicts nature.
13. Practical uses of the
shape-channel approach
13.1 Automated mechanism diagnosis
In a kinematic chain or
articulated machine, construct the local constraint Jacobian, identify rank
defects, distinguish trivial rigid motions from transverse modes, and compute
higher-order compatibility where first order is blind. Use symmetry to reduce
equivalent endpoint exchanges. The ideal polymer results give working examples
of an endpoint exchange that cannot run and a crankshaft that can. A practical
implementation would return the exact constraints, the proposed motion, its
inverse, a local/continuous certificate, and the remaining collision risk.
13.2 Molecular conformational analysis
Begin with
experimentally supported bond geometry and torsional states. Decompile which
local exchanges are kinematically possible without bond or angle distortion;
then attach independently measured energy weights to predict populations and
chain observables. This prevents the common mistake of interpreting a
geometrically valid endpoint exchange as a low-energy physical pathway. For
polyethylene, the next implementation should jointly read  and a trans/gauche population, quantify
sensitivity and model misspecification, and compare out-of-sample temperatures.
13.3 Instrument and sensor design
In a symmetry-rich system, a
null invariant readout may conceal a nonzero antisymmetric or
higher-representation component. Instead of increasing raw sensor count,
calculate the representation content of the target deficiency and choose a
reader with a nonzero response in that sector. Where the first linear reader is
silent, a permitted nonlinear coupling may export part of the hidden channel,
but its sign/phase ambiguity must be stated. This is a concrete observability
strategy rather than a general appeal to “more resolution.”
13.4 Computational verification
and compiler tooling
A route carries
its own inverse or an explicit statement of lost information. Quotient maps
disclose which differences were discarded and what extra context an inverse
needs. Finite algebraic constraints can then be checked by exact arithmetic,
while numerical geometry requires residual, Jacobian, and collision
certificates. The same discipline applies to reversible internal rounds of
cryptographic primitives, but does not turn a compressed digest into an
invertible map. Reversing a SHA-256 round with full state is not solving an
unrestricted SHA-256 preimage [1,3].
13.5 Structural search replacement
For a gap with a known type,
compute the deficiency  before testing candidate fillers. In
semisimple rational settings, its irreducible multiplicities are necessary
method requirements; one faithful-reader span calculation determines whether a
proposed family actually closes it. In physical parameter inference, the
analogous step is to calculate the rank and kernel of the observable Jacobian
before fitting. Both replace blind enumeration with a typed statement of what
the existing measurements can and cannot determine.
14. A prospective empirical
protocol
A
complete physical test of this programme must be designed so failure is
possible. For the first material domain, a credible test would freeze the exact
72-state and reduced four-coordinate readers, the torsion grammar, the
temperature definition, the energy source, and the expected  and  readouts. The prediction and its way home
would be sealed before consulting the selected experimental datasets. A second,
independently obtained trans-fraction readout should be made at matched
thermodynamic conditions. One dataset could be used to select or validate the
constitutive weights, while another temperature or chain-length regime is
withheld for prediction.
The
missing physical law is not resolved merely by adding arbitrary terms until the
characteristic ratio matches. Any extension—bond-angle flexibility, longer
torsional memory, nonbonded interactions, or condensed-phase constraints—must
declare which measured discrepancy requires it and what new observable
distinguishes that extension from the original two-parameter model. Otherwise
the compiler would become a fit engine rather than a decompiler.
The
same protocol generalizes to mechanical linkages: a derived continuous path
should be checked under interval-certified clearance and exact constraints, not
just at finite waypoints. To move beyond molecular mechanics into quantum
matter, a domain mapping would also have to derive complex amplitudes,
Hilbert-space structure, observable algebra, statistics and their measured
constants; none follows from the integer incidence bridge alone.
15. Limitations and
corrections retained in the final theory
This
synthesis retains rather than hides its principal corrections. The original
scalar overlap budget was replaced by an irreducible-by-irreducible capacity
bound; rank equality at an invariant reader was shown not to imply closure
below family granularity. A reader described as faithful may still discard
internal frequency information. The price-blind sphere was traced to an
existing octahedral core, and its zero-weight faces to a coarse reader, not an
absolute cochain-degree obstruction. A wrong finite chain-length bound was
replaced by an exact positive-definite compatibility form. The ideal Gray
traversal was separated from the self-avoiding motion graph. A 13-of-38
diamond-lattice connectivity report became 16-of-38 when a branch crossing was
handled. A proposed uniform Laplacian was shown not uniquely selected by
symmetry; the signed gradient and nonnegative conductance were separated as
distinct objects. Finally, the numerical RIS agreement across two
implementations was separated from experimental agreement.
There
are also important archiving limits. Much of the larger historical ledger
refers to session-local scripts or hashes whose complete source text is not
retained; some claims have been rebuilt and others are provenance-limited [1].
The present paper records results as reported and audited in the supplied
corpus, except where the displayed formulas provide an independent
derivation. Assertions that require a particular source code file, a full
prediction seal, or an unseen raw measurement remain conditional on that
missing material. The full Nexus metaphysical claim—matter as constraint
itself, with no separate external compiler—is a research interpretation. Finite
operator correspondences do not settle it.
16. Conclusion
The Nexus
programme has reached a materially different stage from a collection of
analogies about binary numbers. Its exact objects are now paired addresses,
reversible routes, boundary operators, representation-valued deficiencies,
reader channels, topology of permitted continuation, and physical observables.
The key operational discovery is not a particular numeric constant. It is a
reproducible direction of derivation: start with a relation, read the
methods that relation supports, distinguish addressable from executable
transformations, and use the missing output’s shape to identify the next
independent readout.
Three findings make
this direction concrete. First, the native palindrome-aware partner ring has an
exact, invertible ideal-chain shape reader, including a head-to-tail reversal
law and rigid suffix half-turn dictionary. Second, the two-token relation ledger
demonstrates that an apparent zero or complete readout may arise from the
reader rather than from missing physical or mathematical structure; a
deficiency must be typed and closure certified. Third, tetrahedral geometry and
an energy-weighted RIS transfer take the same method-first logic into a real
physical domain: known polymer rearrangements emerge under declared
constraints, and the chain-size calculation becomes quantitative. Its
discrepancy with external data—and the rank-two requirement for independent
physical parameter identification—is a scientifically useful unresolved
result, not something to conceal.
The emerging system
is best characterized as a relational decompiler with explicit observability
and admissibility contracts. It offers concrete tools for kinematics,
polymer modeling, measurement design and computational verification. Whether
those tools can be extended into a uniquely predictive theory of matter will
depend on closing the constitutive and quantum links without fitting or
inventing them. Until then, the strongest defensible formulation is simple: what
transfers between domains is the shape of the operation—the in-between—not the
label or value of the thing being read.

Appendix A. Operational
contracts for a reusable decompiler
A
decompiler intended to transfer this work beyond the original arena should
accept (i) a typed configuration domain ; (ii) an explicit local action and
inverse or recovery route; (iii) boundary and admissibility predicates; (iv) a
relation vocabulary with an exact or controlled numerical basis; (v) a reader
with a declared kernel or visibility profile; and (vi) a falsifiable measured
target. It should return separately: address equivalence, algebraic
closure, local infinitesimal mobility, second- or higher-order compatibility,
global continuous reachability, and empirical agreement. No one of these
certificates is a substitute for another.
For
a finite group-stable rational relation space, the execution route is:
construct , calculate , identify its irreducible content,
use the reader character to determine visible deficits, and certify a proposed
closing method by an actual span equality. For an ideal physical chain, replace
relation-cell rows with bond-length, angle and contact constraints; preserve
actual geometric inverses; then check singular branches and clearance. For a
material measurement, construct the observable transfer, reduce by symmetry,
and compute the rank of the inverse parameter reader before interpreting
its numerical mismatch.

EXPANDED TECHNICAL APPENDICES · 24 OCTOBER 2026
This edition retains the original mathematical paper
and adds the reversible-ring, stream, steric-geometry and inverse-readout
machinery supplied in the subsequent technical synthesis. Claims absent
independent audit in this revision are attributed to the project ledger; no
retrospective result is recast as a sealed prospective physical prediction.
Appendix C. The native ring as an operational machine: pairing, clock, and
direct addresses
C.1. Address geometry before interpretation
The expanded October record supplies a useful distinction
omitted by a bare list of binary words. At LEN n, the word inventory
X_n={0,1}^n is complete, but a word's ring location is determined by an
additional, explicitly declared partner and order construction. Let J denote
bit reversal and C bitwise complement. The native involution is P(x)=J(x) off
the palindromic set and P(x)=C(x) on that set. Its fixed-point-free pairing is
a statement about the word-domain operation, not a claim that physical systems are
made of binary strings. Each canonical pair contributes one representative to
the left ring face and one partner to the opposing face. Ring-index opposition
expresses the native facing law R_n[N−1−i]=P(R_n[i]), where N=2^n; the seam
exchange is part of the specified address convention.
The construction cannot be simplified into “every
canonical representative starts with zero.” A canonical pair can have two
MSB-one members when the outer bits agree and an interior reversal wins the
comparison. The reported exact count of MSB-one canonical representatives is
2^(n−3)−2^⌊(n−3)/2⌋ for n≥3. I independently enumerated the partner map for
n=3,…,12 in this revision; the predicted sequence 0,1,2,6,12,28,56,120,240,496
matches the observed counts at every width. This matters operationally: a direct-address
reader that assumes one hemisphere equals one MSB value returns incorrect
addresses, despite preserving the coarse two-face picture.
The project record develops palindrome-aware rank/unrank
formulas and a center-out E/G lift for accessing large rings without
materializing 2^n words. The source reports exhaustive low-width checks and
direct high-LEN queries, including n=64 and beyond. Those results are valuable
to the decompiler because the location is computed from the relation grammar
rather than searched in an enumeration. The asymptotic claim of linear-in-width
addressing depends on the implementation and arithmetic model; it is not a
physical time-complexity measurement.
C.2. One Inverter: a deterministic twisted return
The single-bit continuation without a fixed point is NOT.
Applying this local operation only to the bit that exits a cyclic register
gives the affine twisted rotation
τ(x₀x₁…xₙ₋₁) = x₁x₂…xₙ₋₁ ¬x₀;       τⁿ = C;       τ²ⁿ = I.
This is an exact finite operator identity. After n steps
the observable word lies on its complementary face; after 2n it returns. At n=2
the orbit 00→01→11→10→00 is a Gray quarter-turn. Thus a closed return in the
complement quotient need not be a return to the same word. That distinction
becomes useful when interpreting a mirrored physical endpoint: quotient closure
is weaker than a continuous lift between distinct endpoints.
At width three, P and C cease to be the same permutation,
and the four operations {I,P,C,PC} form a Klein four-group on an appropriate
four-element orbit. This is an internal symmetry of the ring. Its natural
even/odd character channels connect the native ring to the later parity
decomposition of ideal-chain shape readers. The group action is an algebraic
identification; no independent spatial SO(3) dynamics or quantum spin
representation follows merely from its existence.
The reported odd-lane alternating-complement lift Λ_q (q
odd) interlaces a smaller s-bit word with its complement in q blocks and
satisfies τ_{qs}Λ_q=Λ_qτ_s and P_{qs}Λ_q=Λ_qP_s. I tested the τ and P
intertwining equations directly for all s=1,…,5 and q∈{3,5}; they hold. The
operation is a precise example of a smaller running ring embedded in a larger
address domain while retaining its methods. This is stronger than observing a
recurring numerical pattern, but still belongs to finite reversible word dynamics.
C.3. The square-root bridge and a caution about its interpretation
Let T advance one position on the specified partner ring,
and form its complement-conjugate T_C=CTC. The algebra establishes PTP=T⁻¹. The
supplemental audit reports a stronger operator comparison: the two permutation
matrices T and T_C have no common directed image, so their Frobenius inner
product counts zero shared arrows on every audited width. Their difference is
orthogonal in the permutation-matrix inner-product sense, not a universal
90-degree angle of physical trajectories.
Define B_op=T_C T⁻¹. The reported bridge gives B_op=S² for
the half-step S=PC T⁻¹. Since C and P commute, the factorization follows by
composition using PTP=T⁻¹; it is an algebraic square root of a relative
permutation, not a unique square root of a physical Hamiltonian. The cycle
census records an origin-to-seam spine at widths 2–5 and detached complementary
branches starting at n=6. Additional symbolic branch certificates describe
16-state and 28-state rung families at high widths. These provide useful routing
and invariant substructures, provided the declared address ordering remains
fixed.
Appendix D. Stream duality and the importance of a retained return
D.1. What the difference reader retains
For even n, the partner-fold difference D(x)=x⊕P(x) is a
palindrome and is nonzero. The zero residual is excluded by the native
fixed-point-free partner definition; the palindromicity follows because P is
either reversal or complement on a palindrome. At n=64 the difference has only
32 independent mirrored positions, even though the original word has 64
positions. The record identifies 2³²−1 distinct nonzero residuals, with a
special doubled preimage count at the all-ones residual. This is a concrete quotient:
its compressed readout deliberately forgets the face and part of the input
distinction.
The inverse is not recovered from the difference alone. As
in the pairwise difference law (a,b)↔(a,a⊕b), one must retain the appropriate
reference or phase coordinate. “Way home” in this paper means a specified
recovery interface or an explicit declaration of information loss, never an
assertion that every many-to-one output has a unique inverse.
D.2. Tagged shifts have a complete local inverse
Let x be a 64-bit aperture and write it as x=a∥y, with a
the departing two-bit prefix and y the remaining 62 bits. An incoming pair b
produces x′=y∥b. The map (x,b)↦x′ is many-to-one if a is discarded, but
(x,b)↦(x′,a) is invertible. Its inverse reconstructs x=a∥(x′≫2) and b=x′ mod 4.
This is a genuine reversible stream read, independent of the semantic content
of the bytes.
In the supplied audit, two named ASCII streams yield 216
two-bit-stride transitions; 169 of those transitions have a different ring jump
from the counterfactual transition obtained by appending 00. The outermost
unequal mirrored pair determines which orientation wins the canonical
comparison, while the innermost unequal mirrored pair determines where an inner
palindrome emerges. These are address-selection and quotient-boundary readers,
respectively. The observed long jumps show that sequential shifts in an input
stream need not be nearest-neighbor steps of T. This prevents the common
substitution of input chronology for native ring adjacency.
Appendix E. From the planar grammar to physically executable paths
E.1. A valid address is not a valid kinetic edge
The six-bead lattice-catalog experiment embeds six legal
conformations in an eight-address register. A cyclic address-hopping term
necessarily crosses the legal/illegal partition, even when the invalid
addresses carry high finite penalties. In contrast, the honeycomb
representation maps each m-bit turn word directly to the (m+2)-bead ideal
chain. The legal subset is selected by self-avoidance on the resulting
coordinates. Its definition is a precondition on execution, not an assignment
of “invalidity” to a numerical address.
The ideal-chain reader decomposes a turn word into a
complementary frame bit and adjacent XOR differences. The differences determine
ordered intrinsic distances in the tested model; the frame determines the
facing embedding. A native ring partner reverses the differences, corresponding
to head-to-tail reversal of the ordered chain shape. Suffix complement
operators implement rigid half-turns about selected bonds in three dimensions.
These relations give a complete kinematic dictionary for the ideal geometry
without asserting that a chosen Gray ring successor is itself an admissible
physical trajectory.
E.2. The methods form a graded precondition hierarchy
The supplemental honeycomb audit details a hierarchy
beyond the earlier paper's short treatment: end-limited moves, conditional
four-turn hexagon rewrites, multihexagon 10-cycle exchanges, and larger end
moves act on different restricted move graphs. Algebraic GF(2) mask
reachability is necessary but not sufficient: a move must also satisfy its
local substring precondition and post-move steric conditions. The zigzag family
Z_m forms a closed subdomain under the both2 vocabulary; boundary-accessible
10-cycle methods can remove that particular closure, while a different class of
alternating loops survives some symmetric extensions. The both3 vocabulary is
reported connected at m=4,…,21, with the first small isolated symmetry orbit
appearing at m=22; no claim of all-width connectedness follows.
These data are important because they locate an
obstruction's owner. If a configuration fails to connect, the cause may be an
absent generator, a generator's unmet local precondition, an excluded collision
state, a restricted boundary, or a genuinely disconnected continuous
configuration space. Reporting only the number of graph components loses that
distinction. The decompiler output must include the generator vocabulary and
its domain of validity.
E.3. A bypass can be executable without a planar waypoint
The two-control-torsion identity e_j=D_j⊕D_{j+1} expresses
one local bit flip as a composition of two physical half-turn operations, but
their schedule matters. Executing them serially may force a planar intermediate
with a steric collision. A simultaneous path in the control torus
(φ₁,φ₂)=(θ,±θ) can avoid that planar corner. The source reports 68
endpoint-valid terminal gates in the m=23–26 residual-lock set, including 12
for which both serial planar intermediates are blocked, and clearance
certificates for synchronized three-dimensional schedules.
This is a constructive local mechanism in the declared
ideal chain model. A finite verified set of swept moves is not evidence that
every physical polymer rearrangement works by the same route. Steric radii,
side groups, barriers, dynamic rates, solvents, and the fixed-exterior
assumptions must remain attached to any transfer into real chemistry.
Appendix F. Higher-order rigidity and diamond-lattice mirror geometry
F.1. First-order flatness does not decide mobility
At a planar configuration of a spatial linkage, normal
displacement columns of the first constraint Jacobian may vanish automatically.
A large tangent nullspace can therefore report apparent freedom even when no
nontrivial exact path exists. The appropriate next read is the second-order
compatibility form: for a first-order tangent z, the quadratic residual q(z)
must be orthogonal to every left-null vector of the Jacobian. The 12-cycle
collinear crankshaft in the ledger has an explicit positive-definite sum-of-squares
combination after factoring out its rigid rotation, closing the nonrigid local
branch at second order. This supersedes the earlier invalid span-bound
explanation.
A separate contact example has squared-distance residual
c(θ)=3(1−cosθ), so its derivative vanishes at θ=0 although its second
derivative is 3. For a specifically declared energy U=(η/2)c², the first
nonzero restoring term is (9η/8)θ⁴. That quartic law is conditional on the
chosen energy penalty, not an automatic prediction of a real contact potential.
Distinguishing contact (one generic scalar constraint) from point welding
(three) or full pose docking (six) avoids an artificial rigidity count.
F.2. Boyer and Schatzki are discriminated by allowed motion, not their
names
The tetrahedral diamond-lattice model provides a more
physically relevant calibration because three fixed torsion states and exact
tetrahedral geometry are inputs. An endpoint exchange of the shortest Boyer
type exists as a pair of conformations but is isolated under strict
bond-length, valence-angle and fixed-exterior constraints. The Schatzki-type
eight-bead crankshaft has aligned flanking bonds and a proper rigid rotation
through 120 degrees. These are recoveries of known polymer mechanisms, not claims
to have discovered them or to have deduced all dynamical activation barriers.
The corrected seven-bond audit records 16 connected
exchange classes out of 38, not 13. Three missed classes connect through the
all-gauche crossing; the correction depends on a verified branch continuation,
not an altered count of conformational endpoints. Singular points are
associated with consecutive trans torsions and a locally planar zigzag in the
enumerated cases. Such local flatness is a necessary condition in the measured
finite family, not a sufficient condition or universal theorem about tetrahedral
chains.
F.3. Two-sheeted quotient: the exact theorem and the remaining missing link
For the mirror pair with reflection R(x,y,z)=(z,y,x),
releasing the specified boundary bond increases local infinitesimal mobility
from one to two degrees of freedom. The exact backward-heading calculation
finds no configuration fixed by R in the extended constrained space; its
smallest squared endpoint mismatch in the mirror-fixed plane is 8/3. Therefore
R acts freely and the quotient by the two-element reflection group is an
unbranched two-sheeted cover, under the declared smooth configuration-space assumptions.
The conclusion is intentionally limited. A free involution
does not imply that A and RA belong to the same connected component. Their
connection exists precisely if a loop in the quotient has an odd-monodromy lift
starting at A and ending at RA. The twisted binary clock demonstrates the
abstract type of half-return, but
does not provide the missing physical loop. The 50-step constrained
interpolation failing to reach its target does not prove the opposite, either.
The physical odd-loop return remains OPEN. This preserves the shape of the
unresolved relation instead of substituting another trajectory search for it.
Appendix G. Quantitative material response and the falsifiable boundary
G.1. A reduction is not a force law
The ring-to-spring bridge uses the integer incidence row
B=[−1,1]. Its evaluation over F₂ is XOR; over the real numbers it measures
extension x₁−x₀. A translation-invariant symmetric two-site quadratic stiffness
is proportional to BᵀB; once an energy scale and masses are supplied, its
normal-mode structure follows. No nonzero additive embedding F₂→R identifies
the respective field elements. What transfers is the incidence relation and its
compositional algebra, not the physical units, energy scale, or material
chemistry.
The polyethylene RIS calculation extends the same method
to a larger readout. The declared tetrahedral continuation grammar and the
gauche-sign reflection symmetry reduce the spatial correlation problem from 72
states to a four-scalar resolvent. The source gives the exact
independent-torsion result C∞=2(2+σ)/(3σ), including the unweighted case C∞=2,
and a full-model crosscheck at numerical precision. These validate the
reduction for its input model. They do not independently establish that real
polyethylene uses ideal tetrahedral bond angles or precisely three discrete
torsional minima.
G.2. The measured gap cannot be renamed as a landing
With externally sourced illustrative energies E_g=2.1
kJ/mol and E_p=8.4 kJ/mol at 413 K, the declared reduced model gives
C∞=6.229676328. The opposite-gauche-forbidden limit at the same E_g gives C∞=6.715345190. The earlier published
scattering central benchmark is about 7.8, with nonidentical measurement
methods and thermodynamic assumptions. The calculated 6.715345190 is a proved
value at ω=0, but calling it the global maximum for 0≤ω≤1 is stronger than the
available evidence: the supplemental record reports a strictly ordered
1,001-point sweep, which supports monotonicity numerically but does not itself
prove it analytically.
Even if full monotonicity were established, the empirical
discrepancy would remain a property of the specified
ideal-lattice energy model, not a demonstration that the ring has failed or
that it fixes real chemical energies. Flexible bond angles, torsion minima, and
non-nearest-neighbor interactions are possible missing physical dependencies;
none should be silently inserted until independently constrained.
G.3. The inverse gap is a second independent measurement
The parameter pair (E_g,E_p) cannot be uniquely
reconstructed from one chain-size observable. The two-output reader
(C∞,p_trans) has a calculated local Jacobian, in parameters measured in kJ/mol,
J ≈ [[0.790254, 0.134369], [0.052463, 0.003102]],    det(J) ≈ −0.004598.
Its nonzero determinant supplies local mathematical
identifiability in the two-parameter RIS model, while the approximately 140
condition number warns that even modest experimental errors can be amplified.
The independently matched trans fraction has not been supplied for the 413 K
material condition; it is UNLINKED, not replaced by a value from a different
phase of polyethylene.
The first prospective physical test must therefore seal both predicted readouts, preserve the
energy-parameter source, temperature, uncertainty model, and phase
specification, and identify the way home through the independent 72-state
implementation. A second experimental number should decide the two-parameter prediction
before any extension of the constitutive law is allowed.
Appendix H. Practical decompiler workflow and status discipline
The complete operational workflow is: (1) declare the
native domain and generator vocabulary; (2) compute the route product and
inverse or record a quotient kernel; (3) separate available algebraic masks
from executable moves with preconditions and boundaries; (4) reduce by exact
symmetries before enumerating trajectories; (5) find the deficiency or
rank-nullity obstruction; (6) request an independent reader only when the
deficiency is AMBIGUOUS; and (7) seal a falsifiable quantitative readout before
importing target measurements. A route that reaches the correct type of structure but not its material
realization remains OPEN, even when its mathematics STANDS.
The paper's practical implications are consequently
specific. In molecular kinematics, it can return methods that are excluded by a
boundary clamp, permitted by an exact rigid sweep, or unresolved due to a
nonrigid motion component. In mechanism design, it can distinguish first-order
zero modes from executable flexes and identify the missing higher-order
constraint. In sensor design, it can recommend which symmetry sector a
measurement must resolve. In computational verification, it can distinguish a
reversible internal state route from a many-to-one published output. In polymer
spectroscopy, it can select observables that remove parameter ambiguity before
any fitting campaign. In every case, the decompiler is useful only to the
extent that the chosen operator relations genuinely describe the physical
domain.

for this expanded edition. STANDS: fixed-point-free partner involution,

quotient-cover/odd-monodromy criterion, and local inverse-function rank test.
RUNS (independently checked during this revision): the MSB-one canonical

for s=1…5 and q=3,5. RUNS (as recorded in the supplied project audit, not all
